# trust-json corpus manifest — the acceptance gate's SAMPLE, pinned.
#
# One checked-in JSON document per line, path relative to the repository root,
# sorted. `tests/acceptance_gate.rs` parses every one of these with BOTH this
# crate and the incumbent `serde_json`, and requires the two to agree on the
# parse, on the tree, and on the exact BYTES of both the compact and the pretty
# re-serialization.
#
# REGENERATE (and read the diff before committing it):
#
#   git ls-files -z -- '*.json' \
#     | tr '\0' '\n' \
#     | grep -v '^first-party/' | grep -v '^vendor/' \
#     | while read -r f; do [ "$(stat -f %z "$f")" -lt 1048576 ] && echo "$f"; done \
#     | sort
#
# SCOPE, and why it stops where it does.
#
#   * `git ls-files`, not `find`: checked-in documents only. Everything under
#     `build/`, `target/` and the various `*.gitignore`d dumps is machine- and
#     moment-specific, and a corpus that changes when you build is not a pin.
#   * `first-party/` is EXCLUDED. Those are submodules; their content changes
#     whenever a gitlink moves, which would turn every pin bump into a red gate
#     for reasons that have nothing to do with a JSON reader.
#   * `vendor/` is EXCLUDED for the same reason.
#   * Documents of 1 MiB or more are EXCLUDED. Measured 2026-08-23: the tracked
#     `*.json` set is 3217 files / 1135.4 MiB, and 263 of those files carry
#     1023.2 MiB of it — 90% of the bytes in 8% of the files. Every one of the
#     263 is a machine-generated dump (`reports/trust-ir-producer-baseline-*`
#     675.5 MiB / 178 files, `crates/trust-clean/fixtures/*` 352.2 MiB / 2338
#     files of which the large tail, `src/llvm-project` 27.9 MiB / 161 files),
#     i.e. the same handful of row shapes repeated a million times. They add
#     minutes of wall clock and no grammar the remaining 2954 files do not
#     already carry — which is not an assertion, it is checked:
#     `corpus_still_exercises_every_measured_construct` puts a floor under each
#     construct this crate can get wrong, and that test reads THIS list.
#
# WHY THIS FILE HOLDS PATHS AND NOT PER-FILE DIGESTS.
#
# The same reasoning `crates/trust-toml/tests/corpus_manifest.txt` records, and
# it applies with more force here: these 2954 documents include compiler test
# fixtures, coverage dumps and scorecards that are rewritten by ordinary work in
# this tree, so a digest list would be red most mornings and the only available
# response would be mechanical regeneration. A gate whose refresh is a reflex is
# not evidence.
#
# What the sample-shift lesson is actually about is the SAMPLE — files
# appearing and, far worse, quietly DISAPPEARING, so that a shrinking corpus
# reads as a green run. That property is delivered in full by pinning the path
# list: `corpus_membership_matches_the_pinned_manifest` recomputes the list from
# `git ls-files` under exactly the rule above and fails on any difference in
# either direction, printing the added and removed paths.
#
# MEASURED AT PIN TIME (2026-08-23): 2954 documents, 117659513 bytes (112.2 MiB).
